A single fabricated claim caps a section regardless of everything else, and four claims here are contradicted by another page on the same domain — not merely unsourced, but actively refuted one click away. Putting the status inside the badge lifts the cap, because an accurate statement about a future certification is not a false statement about a present one.

- — §07 Why publish the roadmap at all. A buyer already in conversation, and an assistant asked “is Margo working towards IATF?”, both get a clear answer with a timeframe instead of silence. The bracketed status keeps the two questions separate — what is held today, and what is coming — without ever presenting the second as the first.  
  _na: commentary_
- — §07 Why it is worse than a ranking problem. Per the AI-citation audit, an assistant that finds a certification claimed flatly on one page and denied on another resolves the conflict by dropping the source, not by picking one. That is why every status now reads identically on all four pages that carry one. An overclaim risks silent removal from the answer set — strictly worse than having said nothing.  
  _na: commentary_

In Progress rows are visible text only.
